DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player guides a constantly advancing dot through a three‑lane tunnel, tapping to shift the dot up one lane (wrapping around) and dodge lane‑specific obstacles. The satisfaction comes from the rhythmic flow of anticipating patterns and executing split‑second lane changes.

## Player
The avatar is a simple glowing dot that moves forward automatically. The player can tap or press a key to shift the dot up one lane, wrapping to the bottom lane when necessary. **Verb:** shift lane.

## Core loop
Obstacles appear ahead in each lane as the dot moves forward. The player watches the upcoming pattern, decides whether to stay or shift, and taps exactly when a lane becomes safe. Every few moments a new set of obstacles arrives, demanding continuous attention and timing.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Stationary blocks** that occupy a lane for a short distance, forcing the player to be in a different lane.
- **Sweeping barriers** that slide horizontally within a lane, opening and closing gaps.
- **Pulsing lasers** that flash on and off, creating timing windows.
- **Spike traps** that pop up briefly, requiring a lane change to avoid.
- **Full‑lane walls** that briefly close an entire lane, making the timing of a lane shift critical.

## Score
- Each obstacle the player passes without collision awards points.
- Perfectly timed lane changes that occur just before a hazard clears grant a bonus.
- Collectible floating orbs that appear in safe lanes add extra points.

## Win and lose
- **Lose:** Colliding with any obstacle triggers a game‑over state showing the final score and a restart prompt.
- **Win:** Surviving the final wave of obstacles (the tunnel’s end) celebrates the player with a burst of visual and audio feedback.

## Feel
The game feels fast‑paced and rhythmic, bathed in neon‑glow visuals that emphasize the three‑lane tunnel. A synth‑driven soundtrack provides a steady pulse, with sharp beeps for lane changes and a crisp crash for collisions. The overall vibe is frantic yet precise, encouraging players to find a flow state.

## Why it's fun
Nailing a split‑second lane swap just as a wall rushes past delivers an instant, satisfying “clear” moment that makes the player grin or shout.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold & meta.json — create index.html with header, canvas, restart button, empty script block; add meta.json matching title, description, controls, tags.
2. Game state initialization — define player, hazards array, score, phase, constants, and input set at top of script.
3. Main loop setup — implement startLoop with cancelAnimationFrame, requestAnimationFrame loop, try/catch, clear canvas each frame.
4. Input handling — add keydown/keyup listeners to populate keys Set; map lane‑shift key and R‑key restart; add click listener on restart button; implement reset() that re‑initializes state and restarts loop.
5. Player movement — implement lane shifting (wrap around three lanes) on input, update player position each frame, expose via window.__game.
6. Hazard system — create hazard objects, spawn logic respecting SAFE_RADIUS and initial safety period, move hazards toward player using dt‑scaled motion.
7. Collision detection — detect player‑hazard overlap, on hit transition to "gameover" phase and stop gameplay.
8. Scoring — increase score on passed hazards or successful lane changes, render score on canvas top‑left.
9. Rendering — clear canvas, draw background, lanes, hazards, player (high‑contrast neon), and HUD each frame.
10. Game‑over overlay — dim background, draw "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions.
11. Observable contract — populate window.__game each frame with state, score, player.x/y/visible, and objective string.
10. Audio — lazy‑init AudioContext, add beep() helper, play sounds for lane shift, score, and collision events.

// === STATE ==============================================================
let loopId = null;
let lastTime = performance.now();
// === LOOP ==============================================================
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      // clear canvas each frame
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // TODO: update and render

      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

Common interpretations:
- "canvas appears blank" → the game is loading but `render()` isn't drawing anything. Check that `startLoop()` actually got called at boot, that `render()` runs every frame, and that you're issuing `ctx.fill*` / `ctx.stroke*` calls per entity each frame (not just clearing the canvas).
- "controls list mismatch" → the `<ul class="game-controls">` in `index.html` and the `controls` array in `meta.json` disagree. Pick one source of truth, edit the other to match exactly.
- "playtest: player.visible was false for X% of frames" → the camera/position math is letting the player drift outside `[0, canvas.width) × [0, canvas.height)`. Either clamp the player or update `player.visible` to reflect the truth (don't just hardcode `true`).
- "playtest: state took invalid value" → `window.__game.state` must be exactly `"menu"`, `"playing"`, or `"gameover"`. No other strings.
- "Required file missing: meta.json" → you didn't write the file. Use your write tool now.
- "pageerror" / "console.error" → an exception is being thrown at runtime. Read the message, locate the line, fix the bug.

**Use your write/edit tool to apply the fixes.** Do NOT respond with code in chat — the files on disk are what gets validated.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called write/edit and that the files on disk now reflect your fixes.

prompt (12,522 chars)
## Reference example: "snake"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Snake",
  "description": "Eat the food. Don't bite yourself or hit the walls. The snake speeds up as you grow.",
  "controls": [
    "Arrow keys or WASD to turn",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "snake", "grid"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Snake</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; image-rendering: pixelated; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Snake</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Eat the food. Don't bite yourself or hit the walls. The snake speeds up as you grow.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or WASD to turn</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const CELL = 25;
  const COLS = 32;            // 32 * 25 = 800
  const ROWS = 24;            // 24 * 25 = 600
  const TICK_START_MS = 140;  // snake step interval at game start
  const TICK_MIN_MS = 60;     // floor speed
  const TICK_SPEEDUP = 3;     // ms shaved off per food eaten

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let snake;          // array of { x, y } in grid coords; snake[0] is head
  let dir;            // { x, y } unit vector
  let nextDir;        // queued direction (so quick double-taps don't U-turn)
  let food;           // { x, y } in grid coords
  let score;
  let highScore = 0;
  let stepIntervalMs;
  let stepAccumulatorMs;
  let phase;          // "playing" | "gameover"
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    snake = [{ x: 10, y: 12 }, { x: 9, y: 12 }, { x: 8, y: 12 }];
    dir = { x: 1, y: 0 };
    nextDir = dir;
    food = randomFreeCell();
    score = 0;
    stepIntervalMs = TICK_START_MS;
    stepAccumulatorMs = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;
    stepAccumulatorMs += dt * 1000;
    while (stepAccumulatorMs >= stepIntervalMs) {
      stepAccumulatorMs -= stepIntervalMs;
      step();
      if (phase !== "playing") return;
    }
  }

  function step() {
    // Commit queued direction; reject reversals.
    if (nextDir.x !== -dir.x || nextDir.y !== -dir.y) dir = nextDir;

    const head = snake[0];
    const nx = head.x + dir.x;
    const ny = head.y + dir.y;

    // Wall collision -> game over.
    if (nx < 0 || nx >= COLS || ny < 0 || ny >= ROWS) {
      gameOver();
      return;
    }
    // Self collision (skip last segment because it's about to move).
    for (let i = 0; i < snake.length - 1; i++) {
      if (snake[i].x === nx && snake[i].y === ny) {
        gameOver();
        return;
      }
    }

    snake.unshift({ x: nx, y: ny });

    if (nx === food.x && ny === food.y) {
      score++;
      stepIntervalMs = Math.max(TICK_MIN_MS, stepIntervalMs - TICK_SPEEDUP);
      food = randomFreeCell();
      beep(660, 0.06);
    } else {
      snake.pop();
    }
  }

  function gameOver() {
    phase = "gameover";
    if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
    beep(120, 0.18);
  }

  function randomFreeCell() {
    while (true) {
      const c = { x: Math.floor(Math.random() * COLS), y: Math.floor(Math.random() * ROWS) };
      if (!snake.some((s) => s.x === c.x && s.y === c.y)) return c;
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    // Background grid.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(125,249,255,0.04)";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    for (let x = 0; x <= COLS; x++) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x * CELL, 0); ctx.lineTo(x * CELL, ROWS * CELL);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    for (let y = 0; y <= ROWS; y++) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, y * CELL); ctx.lineTo(COLS * CELL, y * CELL);
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    // Food (pulsing).
    const pulse = 0.85 + 0.15 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 180);
    drawCell(food.x, food.y, `rgba(255,82,174,${pulse.toFixed(3)})`);

    // Snake body.
    for (let i = snake.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      drawCell(snake[i].x, snake[i].y, "#52ffae");
    }
    // Snake head — brighter so the player tracks it instantly.
    drawCell(snake[0].x, snake[0].y, "#9dffd1");

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}` + (score === highScore && score > 0 ? " — new best!" : ""), canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 16);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 44);
    }
  }

  function drawCell(cx, cy, fill) {
    ctx.fillStyle = fill;
    ctx.fillRect(cx * CELL + 1, cy * CELL + 1, CELL - 2, CELL - 2);
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    if (phase !== "playing") return;
    switch (ev.key) {
      case "ArrowUp": case "w": case "W": nextDir = { x: 0, y: -1 }; break;
      case "ArrowDown": case "s": case "S": nextDir = { x: 0, y: 1 }; break;
      case "ArrowLeft": case "a": case "A": nextDir = { x: -1, y: 0 }; break;
      case "ArrowRight": case "d": case "D": nextDir = { x: 1, y: 0 }; break;
    }
  });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.06, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    const head = snake[0];
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,                     // "playing" | "gameover"
      score,
      player: {
        x: head.x * CELL + CELL / 2,    // canvas-space center
        y: head.y * CELL + CELL / 2,
        visible: head.x >= 0 && head.x < COLS && head.y >= 0 && head.y < ROWS,
      },
      objective: "Eat the pink food. Don't hit the walls or your own tail.",
    };
  }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```js
// === CONFIG =============================================================
const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
```
```js
// === STATE ==============================================================
let loopId = null;
```
let lastTime = performance.now();
```
// === LOOP ==============================================================
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      // clear canvas each frame
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // TODO: update and render
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
```

function reset() {
  // reinitialize
  player = { lane: 1, x: PLAYER_X };
  hazards = [];
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  spawnTimer = 0;
  lastTime = performance.now();
  publishState();
  startLoop();
}

Define update(dt) function: called inside loop.

function update(dt) { if (phase !== "playing") return; // accumulate spawnTimer.

spawnTimer += dt;
while (spawnTimer >= SPAWN_INTERVAL) {
  spawnTimer -= SPAWN_INTERVAL;
  spawnHazard();
}
 // move hazards
 for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   const h = hazards[i];
   h.x -= HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
   // check passed player without collision
   if (h.x + HAZARD_WIDTH < PLAYER_X) {
     // passed, increase score, remove
     score++;
     hazards.splice(i, 1);
   } else {
     // collision check: if same lane and overlaps player x range
     if (h.lane === player.lane && h.x < PLAYER_X + PLAYER_RADIUS && h.x + HAZARD_WIDTH > PLAYER_X - PLAYER_RADIUS) {
       // collision
       gameOver();
       break;
     }
   }
 }

 // handle lane shift input
 if (keys.has("Space") || keys.has("Enter")) {
   // shift lane once per keypress? using nextDir flag if needed.

Thus render can:

```js
function render() {
  // background
  ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // lanes
  ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(125,249,255,0.1)";
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  for (let i = 1; i < LANE_COUNT; i++) {
    const y = i * LANE_HEIGHT;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, y);
    ctx.lineTo(canvas.width, y);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
  hazards.forEach(h => {
    const y = h.lane * LANE_HEIGHT + LANE_HEIGHT / 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(h.x, y, PLAYER_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  });

  // player
  const playerY = player.lane * LANE_HEIGHT + LANE_HEIGHT / 2;
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff"; // bright neon
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, playerY, PLAYER_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // HUD: score
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
}
```

We also need to ensure window.__game is populated (task 11 later). Not needed now, but safe.

```js
// === INPUT ==============================================================
document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') {
    reset();
  } else {
    keys.add(ev.key);
  }
});
document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => {
  keys.delete(ev.key);
});
```

// === RESET ==============================================================
function reset() {
  // reinitialize game state
  player.lane = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  score = 0;
  hazards = [];
  lastTime = performance.now();
  startLoop();
}

// === INPUT ==============================================================
const keys = new Set();
document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
  const key = ev.key;
  if (key === 'r' || key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
  // lane shift key: space or ArrowUp
  if (key === ' ' || key === 'Space' || key === 'ArrowUp') {
    keys.add('Shift');
  }
});
document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => {
  const key = ev.key;
  if (key === ' ' || key === 'Space' || key === 'ArrowUp') {
    keys.delete('Shift');
  }
});
document.getElementById('restart').addEventListener('click', reset);

// === UPDATE ==============================================================
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== 'playing') return;
  // lane shift logic
  if (keys.has('Shift')) {
    // shift lane up (wrap)
    player.lane = (player.lane + 1) % LANES;
    // reset key so it doesn't shift continuously
    keys.delete('Shift');
    // placeholder for score increase for lane shift (optional)
    score += 1;
  }
  // placeholder: no hazards movement yet
}

// === RENDER ==============================================================
function render() {
  // background
  ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  // draw lanes (optional grid)
  ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(125,249,255,0.04)";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let i = 1; i < LANES; i++) {
    const y = i * LANE_HEIGHT;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, y);
    ctx.lineTo(canvas.width, y);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  // draw player dot
  const playerX = canvas.width / 2;
  const playerY = (player.lane + 0.5) * LANE_HEIGHT;
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(playerX, playerY, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  // HUD - score
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
  // Game Over overlay
  if (phase === 'gameover') {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillText("Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 16);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 44);
  }
}

// === BOOT ===============================================================
startLoop();

prompt (5,137 chars)
Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Lane Hopper
Concept: The dot auto‑advances through a three‑lane tunnel; each tap moves it up one lane (wrapping around) to avoid lane‑specific obstacles, demanding rhythmic lane changes to survive.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.
